<p>For nearly a decade, Communication Studies Adjunct Faculty member Meg Bucaro Wojtas has been empowering students to communicate more confidently and comfortably. She came to Elgin Community College in 2009 because, as an Elgin resident, she was well aware of the growth the college was experiencing at the time and wanted to be part of the excitement.</p>

</h3>
<p>Diane Kerruish, senior executive assistant to the president, has seen her fair share of change during her eleven-year tenure at ECC. Her first experience at the college was as a student, and later she returned as the assistant to the managing director of marketing and communications. The ECC experience that truly changed her life, though, was meeting her husband while playing intramural softball!</p>

<p>Elgin Community College’s We Rise Gala: A Community Fundraiser for Undocumented, DACA, and Mixed Status Families will take place Friday, Nov. 15. The rescheduled Latinx Heritage Month closing event will run from 6 to 10:30 p.m. in the Jobe Lounge, Building B, 1700 Spartan Drive, Elgin.

<h3 class="h4 headline">
<a href="/news-events/ecc-in-the-news/2019/eccs-student-newspaper-the-observer-wins-2nd-place-best-in-show-award.aspx">ECC’s student newspaper, the Observer, wins 2nd place Best in Show Award</a>
</h3>
<p>At the Illinois Community College Journalism Association conference held Friday, Oct. 4, the Elgin Community College student newspaper, Observer, won second place in the John H. Ryan Best in Show competition for the May 13 issue. This annual award recognizes the best single print issues published during the spring semester among all community colleges in Illinois. </p>
<div class="separate"><a class="btn btn-primary" href="/news-events/ecc-in-the-news/2019/eccs-student-newspaper-the-observer-wins-2nd-place-best-in-show-award.aspx">Read Article</a><a class="btn btn-white" href="/news-events/">All news stories</a></div>

<h2 class="section-header">WE'RE HERE FOR THE COMMUNITY<span></span></h2>
<div class="container">
<div class="row">
<!--<div class="col-md-12 gen-content" id="community">
<h2>WE'RE HERE FOR THE COMMUNITY</h2><span class="e-a3abaf header-hLine header-hLine-thin"></span>
<div class="col-md-12">
  <div class="row">-->
<div class="col-md-3 col-xs-12 e-tiles box-sm bg-005195 e-white" style="">
<h3>Spartan Drive Campus</h3>
<p>Located mid-way between Chicago and Rockford, ECC is easily accessible from U.S. 20 and Interstate 90 in southwest Elgin.</p>
<a class="btn btn-transparent btn-bottom" href="/about-ecc/visiting-campus/campus-map/">Campus Map</a>
</div>
<div class="col-md-3 col-xs-12 e-tiles box-sm bg-003a69 e-white" style="">
<h3>Library</h3>
<p>Borrowing privileges and use of the Renner Academic Library are open to any resident of District 509.</p>
<a class="btn btn-transparent btn-bottom" href="/life-at-ecc/library/">Learn More</a>
</div>
<div class="col-md-3 col-xs-12 e-tiles box-sm bg-007e66 e-white" style="">
<h3>Spartan Terrace</h3>
<p>Cuisine prepared and served by the students of the award winning Culinary Arts and Hospitality Program of ECC.</p>
<a class="btn btn-transparent btn-bottom" href="/life-at-ecc/services-resources/food-services/spartan-terrace/">Learn More</a>
</div>
<div class="col-md-3 col-xs-12 e-tiles box-sm bg-005c56 e-white" style="">
<h3>Career Development</h3>
<p>ECC provides help with employment assistance, available to students and community members.</p>
<a class="btn btn-transparent btn-bottom" href="/life-at-ecc/career-development-services/">Learn More</a>
</div>
</div><div class="row">
<div class="col-md-3 col-xs-12 e-tiles box-sm bg-be0f34 e-white" style="">
<h3>Athletics </h3>
<p>ECC student-athletes compete in regional and national tournaments.</p>
<a class="btn btn-transparent btn-bottom" href="/life-at-ecc/athletics/">Learn More</a>
</div>
<div class="col-md-3 col-xs-12 e-tiles box-sm bg-901c3b e-white" style="">
<h3>Arts Center</h3>
<p>Intimate theatres, dining through our award-winning culinary arts program, easy accommodations, plus free parking.</p>
<a class="btn btn-transparent btn-bottom" href="/life-at-ecc/arts-center/">Learn More</a>
</div>
